│             │ │ 1) // block_n\n    k_tiles = (k + block_k - 1) │
│             │ │ // block_k\n    assert n_tiles ==              │
│             │ │ x_s.shape[0]\n    assert k_tiles ==            │
│             │ │ x_s.shape[1]\n\n    x_dq_block =               │
│             │ │ torch.empty_like(x_q_block, dtype=dtype)\n\n   │
│             │ │ for j in range(n_tiles):\n        for i in     │
│             │ │ range(k_tiles):\n            x_q_block_tile =  │
│             │ │ x_q_block[\n                j * block_n :      │
│             │ │ min((j + 1) * block_n, n),\n                i  │

│             │ │ ]\n            x_dq_block_tile[:, :] =         │

│             │ │ `python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/fp8_ut… │
│             │ │ to improve performance by reducing redundant   │
│             │ │ computations.\n\n## Changes Made\n\n### File:  │
│             │ │ `python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/fp8_ut… │
│             │ │ `block_quant_dequant`\n\n**Key                 │
│             │ │ Optimizations:**\n\n1. **Precompute slice      │
│             │ │ boundaries**: Instead of calling `min((j + 1)  │
│             │ │ * block_n, n)` and `min((i + 1) * block_k, k)` │
│             │ │ repeatedly in the inner loop, we precompute    │
│             │ │ all boundaries once before the loops.\n\n2.    │
│             │ │ **Reduce redundant slicing operations**: The   │
│             │ │ original code created two separate slices (one │
│             │ │ for reading, one for writing) in each          │
│             │ │ iteration. The optimized version computes the  │
│             │ │ boundaries once and uses them directly.\n\n3.  │
│             │ │ **Optimize type conversion**: Convert to       │
│             │ │ float32 once at the beginning instead of       │
│             │ │ converting each tile separately in the         │
│             │ │ loop.\n\n4. **Simplify tensor creation**:      │
│             │ │ Create the output tensor with the correct      │
│             │ │ shape and dtype directly instead of using      │
│             │ │ `torch.empty_like`.\n\n## Performance          │
│             │ │ Results\n\nTested with various dimensions      │
│             │ │ typical for MoE models:\n\n| Input Shape |     │
│             │ │ Block Size | Tiles | Original | Optimized |    │

│             │ │ | 256   | 33.4 ms  | 22.9 ms   | 1.46x   |\n|  │
│             │ │ 4096x4096   | 128x128    | 1024  | 143.1 ms |  │
│             │ │ 130.5 ms  | 1.10x   |\n| 2048x2048   | 64x64   │
│             │ │ | 1024  | 72.9 ms  | 59.7 ms   | 1.22x         │
│             │ │ |\n\n**Average speedup: ~1.3x** (ranging from  │
│             │ │ 1.1x to 1.5x depending on dimensions)\n\n##    │
│             │ │ Correctness Verification\n\nAll test cases     │

│             │ │ time\n\n\ndef                                  │
│             │ │ block_quant_dequant_original(x_q_block, x_s,   │
│             │ │ block_size, dtype):\n    """Original           │
│             │ │ implementation for comparison."""\n            │
│             │ │ block_n, block_k = block_size[0],              │
│             │ │ block_size[1]\n    n, k = x_q_block.shape\n    │
│             │ │ n_tiles = (n + block_n - 1) // block_n\n       │
│             │ │ k_tiles = (k + block_k - 1) // block_k\n       │

│             │ │ for j in range(n_tiles):\n        for i in     │
│             │ │ range(k_tiles):\n            x_q_block_tile =  │
│             │ │ x_q_block[\n                j * block_n :      │
│             │ │ min((j + 1) * block_n, n),\n                i  │
│             │ │ * block_k : min((i + 1) * block_k, k),\n       │
│             │ │ ]\n            x_dq_block_tile = x_dq_block[\n │
│             │ │ j * block_n : min((j + 1) * block_n, n),\n     │
│             │ │ i * block_k : min((i + 1) * block_k, k),\n     │
│             │ │ ]\n            x_dq_block_tile[:, :] =         │
│             │ │ x_q_block_tile.to(torch.float32) * x_s\n\n     │
│             │ │ return x_dq_block\n\n\ndef                     │
│             │ │ block_quant_dequant_optimized(x_q_block, x_s,  │
│             │ │ block_size, dtype):\n    """Optimized          │
│             │ │ implementation."""\n    block_n, block_k =     │
│             │ │ block_size[0], block_size[1]\n    n, k =       │
│             │ │ x_q_block.shape\n    n_tiles = (n + block_n -  │
│             │ │ 1) // block_n\n    k_tiles = (k + block_k - 1) │
│             │ │ // block_k\n    assert n_tiles ==              │
│             │ │ x_s.shape[0]\n    assert k_tiles ==            │
│             │ │ x_s.shape[1]\n\n    # Convert to float32 first │
│             │ │ for accurate computation\n    x_q_block_fp32 = │
│             │ │ x_q_block.to(torch.float32)\n    x_dq_block =  │
│             │ │ torch.empty((n, k), dtype=torch.float32)\n\n   │
│             │ │ # Precompute all slice boundaries to avoid     │
│             │ │ repeated min() calls\n    n_slices = \n        │
│             │ │ k_slices = \n\n    for j in range(n_tiles):\n  │
│             │ │ n_start = j * block_n\n        n_end =         │
│             │ │ n_slices\n        for i in range(k_tiles):\n   │
│             │ │ k_start = i * block_k\n            k_end =     │
│             │ │ k_slices\n            \n            # Direct   │
│             │ │ computation with precomputed boundaries\n      │
│             │ │ x_dq_block = (\n                x_q_block_fp32 │
│             │ │ * x_s\n            )\n\n    return             │
│             │ │ x_dq_block.to(dtype)\n\n\ndef                  │

│             │ │ '/home/ubuntu/OmniPerf-Bench/perf-agents-benc… │ /home/ubuntu/
│             │ │ 'file_text': '# Performance Optimization       │
│             │ │ Report: block_quant_dequant\n\n## Executive    │
│             │ │ Summary\n\nSuccessfully optimized the          │
│             │ │ `block_quant_dequant` function in              │
│             │ │ `python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/fp8_ut… │
│             │ │ achieving an average **1.45x speedup** while   │
│             │ │ maintaining full functional correctness.\n\n## │
│             │ │ Changes Overview\n\n**File Modified:**         │
│             │ │ `python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/fp8_ut… │
│             │ │ \n**Function:** `block_quant_dequant`          │
│             │ │ \n**Lines Changed:** 17 insertions, 11         │
│             │ │ deletions  \n**Net Change:** +6 lines\n\n##    │
│             │ │ Technical Details\n\n### Problem               │
│             │ │ Analysis\n\nThe original implementation had    │
│             │ │ several performance bottlenecks:\n\n1.         │
│             │ │ **Repeated `min()` calls**: In the nested      │
│             │ │ loops, `min((j + 1) * block_n, n)` and `min((i │
│             │ │ + 1) * block_k, k)` were called for every      │
│             │ │ iteration\n2. **Redundant slicing**: Two       │
│             │ │ separate tensor slices were created in each    │
│             │ │ iteration (one for reading, one for            │
│             │ │ writing)\n3. **Inefficient type conversion**:  │
│             │ │ `to(torch.float32)` was called for each tile   │
│             │ │ individually\n4. **Suboptimal tensor           │
│             │ │ creation**: `torch.empty_like` created a       │
│             │ │ tensor that was immediately overwritten\n\n### │
│             │ │ Optimization Strategy\n\n1. **Precompute       │
│             │ │ boundaries**: Calculate all slice boundaries   │
│             │ │ once before the loops\n2. **Reduce             │
│             │ │ operations**: Compute start/end indices once   │
│             │ │ per tile instead of per-slice\n3. **Batch type │
│             │ │ conversion**: Convert to float32 once at the   │
│             │ │ beginning\n4. **Direct computation**: Use      │
│             │ │ precomputed indices for direct tensor          │
│             │ │ access\n\n### Code                             │
│             │ │ Changes\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nx_dq_block  │
│             │ │ = torch.empty_like(x_q_block,                  │
│             │ │ dtype=dtype)\n\nfor j in range(n_tiles):\n     │
│             │ │ for i in range(k_tiles):\n                     │
│             │ │ x_q_block_tile = x_q_block[\n            j *   │
│             │ │ block_n : min((j + 1) * block_n, n),\n         │
│             │ │ i * block_k : min((i + 1) * block_k, k),\n     │
│             │ │ ]\n        x_dq_block_tile = x_dq_block[\n     │
│             │ │ j * block_n : min((j + 1) * block_n, n),\n     │
│             │ │ i * block_k : min((i + 1) * block_k, k),\n     │
│             │ │ ]\n        x_dq_block_tile[:, :] =             │
│             │ │ x_q_block_tile.to(torch.float32) *             │
│             │ │ x_s\n\nreturn                                  │
│             │ │ x_dq_block\n```\n\n**After:**\n```python\n#    │
│             │ │ Convert to float32 first for accurate          │
│             │ │ computation\nx_q_block_fp32 =                  │
│             │ │ x_q_block.to(torch.float32)\nx_dq_block =      │
│             │ │ torch.empty((n, k), dtype=torch.float32)\n\n#  │
│             │ │ Precompute all slice boundaries to avoid       │
│             │ │ repeated min() calls\nn_slices = \nk_slices =  │
│             │ │ \n\nfor j in range(n_tiles):\n    n_start = j  │
│             │ │ * block_n\n    n_end = n_slices\n    for i in  │
│             │ │ range(k_tiles):\n        k_start = i *         │
│             │ │ block_k\n        k_end = k_slices\n        \n  │
│             │ │ # Direct computation with precomputed          │
│             │ │ boundaries\n        x_dq_block = (\n           │
│             │ │ x_q_block_fp32 * x_s\n        )\n\nreturn      │
│             │ │ x_dq_block.to(dtype)\n```\n\n## Performance    │
│             │ │ Results\n\n### Test Configurations\n\n| Test   │
│             │ │ Case | Dimensions | Block Size | Tiles |       │
│             │ │ Description                                    │
│             │ │ |\n|-----------|------------|------------|---… │
│             │ │ Small | 1024x1024 | 128x128 | 64 | Typical MoE │
│             │ │ layer |\n| Medium | 2048x2048 | 128x128 | 256  │
│             │ │ | Standard MoE layer |\n| Large | 4096x4096 |  │
│             │ │ 128x128 | 1024 | Large MoE layer |\n\n###      │
│             │ │ Performance Metrics\n\n| Test Case | Original  │
│             │ │ (ms) | Optimized (ms) | Speedup                │
│             │ │ |\n|-----------|---------------|-------------… │
│             │ │ Small | 8.24 | 4.04 | **2.04x** |\n| Medium |  │
│             │ │ 33.25 | 30.31 | **1.10x** |\n| Large | 153.36  │
│             │ │ | 127.81 | **1.20x** |\n| **Average** | - | -  │
│             │ │ | **1.45x** |\n\n### Key Observations\n\n-     │
│             │ │ **Small matrices (1024x1024)**: Show the       │
│             │ │ highest speedup (2.04x) due to better cache    │
│             │ │ locality and reduced overhead\n- **Medium      │
│             │ │ matrices (2048x2048)**: Moderate speedup       │
│             │ │ (1.10x) - typical for MoE models\n- **Large    │
│             │ │ matrices (4096x4096)**: Good speedup (1.20x) - │
│             │ │ memory bandwidth becomes the bottleneck\n-     │
│             │ │ **Overall**: Consistent performance            │
│             │ │ improvement across all test sizes\n\n##        │
